STATE ELECTION COMMISSION PUNJAB
NOTIFICATION
The 6th January, 2019
NO. SEC-(GP)-SA-2019/190.- In pursuance of the provisions of Section 71 of the Punjab State Election Commission Act, 1994
(Punjab Act No. 19 of 1994) and all other powers enabling him in this behalf , the Election Commission do hereby notifies the persons
elected as Sarpanch or Panch from Block Adampur, Jalandhar East, Jalandhar West, Bhogpur, Shahkot, Lohian Khas, Nakodar,
Mehatpur, Phillaur, Nurmahal and Rurka Kalan of the District Jalandhar as per schedule given below:-
